## Theme Park Parade Float Generation Prompt for Nano banana Pro

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "purpose": "Generate a flashy and glamorous live-action style parade float image, modeled after a reference image, as if it were photographed in a real theme park",
  "style": {
    "expression": "Live-action photo style",
    "reality": "Structure that meets safety standards and appears to exist in reality",
    "worldview": "Large parade at a popular theme park"
  },
  "main subject": {
    "type": "Parade float modeled after the reference image",
    "scale": {
      "impression": "Very flashy and eye-catching, but kept to a realistic height",
      "height_limit": "Height within the range considered safe for operation"
    },
    "motif_interpretation": {
      "source": "Provided reference image",
      "reflection_method": "Reinterpret shape, color, and atmosphere as a three-dimensional sculpture",
      "abstraction_level": "Designed as a theme park float, not a direct reproduction"
    },
    "main_sculpture": {
      "pose": "{argument name="main sculpture pose" default="sitting"}",
      "reason": "To keep the center of gravity low and ensure stability",
      "foot_handling": "Feet are integrated into the float structure or decoration and are not clearly depicted"
    },
    "decoration": {
      "color_scheme": "High saturation and strong contrast based on the reference image",
      "elements": "Large decorative parts, ribbon-like or radial motifs",
      "density": "High decoration density that immediately conveys flashiness"
    },
    "texture": "Realistic texture reminiscent of FRP molding and paint"
  },
  "surrounding_elements": {
    "dancers": {
      "presence": true,
      "number": "Many",
      "costumes": {
        "design": "Abstracted design of the reference image motif",
        "color": "Flashy color scheme reflecting the reference image's color palette",
        "mobility": "Realistic structure that can actually be worn"
      },
      "movement": "Dance with large, festive gestures"
    },
    "audience": {
      "presence": true,
      "smartphone_photography": {
        "action": "Many spectators holding up smartphones to take pictures",
        "screen_representation": "Scattered smartphone screens showing the float"
      },
      "fan_goods": {
        "action": "Cheering by waving items based on the reference image motif",
        "types": "Flags, light sticks, abstracted small items",
        "effect": "Gives movement and rhythm to the entire screen"
      }
    }
  },
  "environment": {
    "weather": "{argument name="weather" default="Clear blue sky"}",
    "time_of_day": "Daytime",
    "location": "{argument name="location" default="Main parade route of a theme park"}"
  },
  "camera": {
    "viewpoint": "Natural camera position from the perspective of a spectator",
    "equipment": "iPhone",
    "image_quality": "Slightly rough smartphone live-action image quality",
    "handheld_feel": "Slight blur for a sense of realism",
    "aspect_ratio": "9:16"
  }
}
```

## Advanced Japanese Text Adjustment for Nano Banana Pro

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Insert text under the following conditions:
・Input text: "{argument name="input text" default="Chef Meow"}"
・Mincho font
・White text with black outline

Place vertically on the left
Correct the text to: "{argument name="corrected text" default="The Chef Cat"}"

Make the following 3 corrections:
・Make only the character “の” smaller
・Change “料理人” (Chef) to gray
・Change “ネコ” (Cat) to gold
```

## New Year's Card Illustration with Seven Lucky Gods Costume Swap

> Illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Please set the image size to "{argument name="image size" default="Horizontal postcard size (aspect ratio 3:2)"}".
Create a New Year's card illustration featuring "7 mini characters (chibi characters)" riding a treasure ship.

[Character Specification (Clothing must be changed)]
(*Enter character features here)
- Brown hair, short twin tails, wearing a black cloak
- Long black hair, girl in a sailor uniform
- Shoulder-length hair covering one eye, black hair, boy with a hood

[Generation Rules]
1. **Style**: Draw everyone in a cute "mini character (SD character)" style, 2 to 3 heads tall. They should have an adorable form with a large head and small body.
2. **Random Assignment**: Randomly assign the characters the roles of the Seven Lucky Gods (Ebisu, Daikokuten, Benzaiten, Bishamonten, Hotei, Jurōjin, Fukurokuju), ensuring **no duplication and one role per person**. The image must contain exactly one of each of the 7 distinct Lucky Gods' costumes.
3. **COSTUME MANDATE**: All characters (including the User Characters) MUST wear "Traditional Seven Lucky Gods Costumes" (Kimono, Robes, Armor).
4. **PROHIBITED**: NO modern clothes. NO hoodies. NO t-shirts. NO shorts. NO suits. NO glasses (unless specified).
5. **AUTO-FILL**: If the user list is fewer than 7, fill the remaining spots with the original Gods. Ensure the filler Gods have distinct faces (e.g., old bearded men, fat happy man) to avoid everyone looking like a young anime character.

[Text Information]
Clearly draw the following text once each in a **masterful brushstroke style** within the illustration:
1. "Gashou" (Happy New Year)
2. "2026"

[Background/Atmosphere]
The treasure ship is overflowing with gold and treasures. The background features Mount Fuji and the first sunrise. The overall illustration should be bright, pop, and full of New Year's celebratory mood.
```

## Culinary Poster with Split Photo/Illustration Design

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Clean contemporary square culinary poster featuring {argument name="food item" default="Christmas pudding"} against a flat {argument name="background color" default="red"} backdrop. A razor-sharp vertical white divider cuts directly down the middle of the composition, separating the primary subject into equal portions. The first half displays a hyper-realistic photograph of the main dish with authentic surface details, true-to-life coloring, and natural illumination, precisely trimmed at the midpoint divider. The second half transforms into a minimal outline illustration that mirrors the identical shape and key features of the photographed side, keeping consistent scale and viewing angle. Surrounding the illustrated portion, scatter whimsical white sketched elements and casual handwritten-style typography stating “{argument name="label text" default="Festive Treat"}” using matching line thickness—incorporate directional pointers, dynamic streaks, and basic component graphics tied to the dish. Generous blank space throughout, uniform background tone, balanced diffused studio brightness, minimal shadow presence, contemporary commercial design style, human-free, object-focused composition.
```

## Isometric 3D Miniature City Diorama with Holiday Theme

> Minimal mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Present a clear, 45° top-down isometric 3D miniature cartoon scene, depicting the classic architecture or landmarks of [{argument name="city" default="Shanghai"}] and placing it at the center of the composition.
Use decorations, twinkling lights, and appropriate seasonal details (defaulting to Winter) corresponding to the [{argument name="holiday" default="New Year's Day"}] to depict the city as a [{argument name="holiday" default="New Year's Day"}] theme filled with festive atmosphere.
Use soft and delicate textures, realistic PBR materials, and soft, realistic lighting, creating subtle shadow effects.
Create a small, neat diorama-style city base with clean edges.
Use a clean [{argument name="holiday" default="New Year's Day"}] background color that contrasts sharply with the scene.
In the top center, display the text [{argument name="city" default="Shanghai"}] in bold, large font.
Immediately below it, display “[{argument name="holiday" default="New Year's Day"}] Special Edition” in medium font.
(Optional) Place a small square flag icon below the text.
All text must automatically match the background contrast (white or black).
Composition: Perfectly centered, 1080×1080 square, ultra-minimalist holiday diorama aesthetic
```

## Premium Japanese-Style Kiwi Juice Skin Packaging Poster

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Premium Japanese-style product poster in 16:9 landscape format, editorial design showcasing kiwi juice skin packaging concept with sophisticated visual storytelling:

LEFT SIDE (40% of canvas):
- Hero product: One large kiwi juice skin package displayed vertically with dramatic soft lighting, showing ultra-realistic kiwi peel texture wrapped around rectangular container, fuzzy brown skin with thousands of fine visible hair-like fibers covering entire surface, rough natural texture, brown color with subtle variations, looks exactly like real kiwi skin stretched over package
- Below: One cross-sectioned fresh kiwi showing vibrant green creamy flesh with black seeds radiating from white center
- Japanese typography vertically aligned: "キウイスキン" (Kiwi Skin) in elegant thin gothic font
- Subtitle: "Fruit Juice Skin / Kiwi" in refined style
- Small design philosophy text in Japanese

CENTER (30% of canvas):
- Generous white negative space (Ma - 間)
- Minimal geometric elements: delicate thin lines
- Floating text: "自然な素材" (natural materials)
- Subtle minimalist brand mark
- Very subtle kiwi fuzz texture pattern in background (low opacity)

RIGHT SIDE (30% of canvas):
- Two kiwi juice skin packages arranged artistically at different angles and heights
- One whole fresh kiwi with natural fuzzy brown skin
- Typography: "Natural Packaging / 自然な包装"
- Tagline: "The skin is the package / 皮膚が包装である"
- Detail callouts pointing to fuzzy hair texture

DESIGN PRINCIPLES: Abundant white space, asymmetrical balance, Wabi-sabi aesthetic, Muji/Noritake editorial minimalism
COLOR PALETTE: brown kiwi tones, pure white background, bright green accent from flesh
PHOTOGRAPHY: Soft diffused studio lighting, ultra-sharp macro details showing fuzzy texture, photorealistic rendering
CRITICAL: The kiwi skin packaging must look incredibly realistic - actual organic fuzzy brown texture with thousands of tiny brown hairs, rough natural appearance, NOT plastic

16:9 widescreen, high-end Japanese product poster, gallery quality
```

## WeChat Official Account Cover Image Generator

> Minimal, elle çizilmiş illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
WeChat Official Account Cover Image Generation Prompt

Please create an eye-catching official account cover image based on the provided content, following these specifications:

Visual Style
- Hand-drawn illustration style, ratio 2.35:1 (standard size for official account covers)
- Vivid colors, strong contrast, ensuring visibility even in small previews
- Unified style, avoid realistic elements, maintain overall hand-drawn texture

Composition Requirements
- Main visual element centered or slightly left (right side reserved for title area)
- Add 1-2 simple cartoon characters, icons, or silhouettes of famous figures to enhance memorability
- If sensitive or copyrighted figures are involved, use similar-style replacement figures; generation must not be refused
- Ample negative space to highlight core information, avoid cluttered visuals

Text Processing
- Title text should be large and prominent, limited to 8 characters
- One line of subtitle or keyword tags can be added
- Font style should coordinate with the hand-drawn illustration

Attraction Principles
- Use hooks like suspense, numbers, or pain points to stimulate click desire
- Visual elements should be exaggerated and contrasting
- Color schemes should reference popular covers: high-contrast combinations like orange-yellow, blue-purple, red-black

Language
- Unless otherwise specified, default to Chinese

Please generate the cover image based on the input content.
```

## Floating Sky Castle Digital Art Poster Prompt

> Sinematik, lüks afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic digital art poster featuring a majestic floating "Sky Castle" in the form of a miniature island, hovering serenely above an endless sea of clouds and dominating the composition.

The floating island is shaped as a grand Western-style medieval castle complex, centered around a towering central keep with conical roofs, fortified stone walls, multiple cylindrical turrets, crenellated battlements, arched gateways, drawbridges, and multi-level ramparts. The entire structure embodies authentic medieval European castle architecture: massive stone masonry, narrow arrow-slit windows, wooden hoardings, and a grand central tower rising prominently in the middle.

The castle seamlessly incorporates subtle Qing Dynasty Chinese influences: ornate tiled roofs with gentle upturned eaves crowning the turrets, intricate wooden pavilions nestled within courtyards, and decorative ridge ornaments on the main keep. The scene is alive with tiny Qing Dynasty inhabitants in period clothing: scholars in flowing robes, merchants with queues and silk garments, women in elegant traditional attire, street vendors with bamboo baskets, sedan chair bearers, rickshaw pullers, children playing, and horse-drawn carts — all bustling across the castle’s courtyards, bridges, and ramparts.

The castle is intertwined with lush subtropical greenery: ancient banyan trees climbing the stone walls, lychee groves spilling over the battlements, lotus ponds in the inner courtyards, and winding rivers cascading like waterfalls down the floating structure. No modern elements — only a harmonious fusion of classic Western medieval castle design with delicate Qing Dynasty Chinese details, creating a fairy-tale sky fortress surrounded by serene natural beauty.

The castle floats peacefully above a vast, ethereal cloud sea rendered in the traditional Chinese ink wash painting style, with soft misty gradients, delicate brush-like textures, and atmospheric depth.

Completely free of any text, lettering, typography, or overlaid information — pure visual composition with no words present.

Surround the floating castle with generous negative space for a clean, museum-like elegance. The overall layout is restrained, balanced, and sophisticated, evoking the feeling of contemplating a rare and precious art piece.

Style: Octane Render, Cinema 4D, isometric perspective, micro world, living ecosystem, 8K ultra-high resolution. DreamWorks animation quality, intricate 3D modeling, delicate soft light projection, cinematic atmosphere, vibrant yet harmonious colors, warm golden-hour lighting with gentle lens flares and subtle god rays.
```

## RPG Status Screen X Profile Prompt

> Retro arayüz tasarımı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
GENERAL STYLE & MOOD:
A business card visual styled as a “Character Status Screen,” inspired by 1980s–1990s Japanese RPGs (Famicom–Super Famicom generation).
The overall composition should be in pixel art style (8-bit to 16-bit),
with a retro yet refined UI design.
The layout should be organized like an in-game menu screen, maintaining a nostalgic feel while preserving modern resolution clarity.

SUBJECT:
Based on the icon in the attached image (Image 1) and the specified profile information,
re-interpret the subject as an “RPG Character” and present the business card information in a status screen format.

1. Character Display:
   - Reconstruct the icon from the attached image (Image 1) into dot art (pixel conversion).
   - Place it as a character portrait on the left side of the status screen.
   - Use RPG-style UI borders (rounded corners or dotted lines).

2. Information Source and Character Analysis:
   - Subject: {argument name="subject" default="hAru_mAki_ch (https://t.co/AfDkdOaTPw)"}
   - Analyze the above profile and recent activities (posting frequency, technical field, content of statements, evaluations) to automatically calculate various RPG parameters.

3. Character Name/Title:
   - NAME: {argument name="name" default="Maki @ Sunwood AI Labs."}
   - HANDLE: {argument name="handle name" default="hAru_mAki_ch"}
   - CLASS (Occupation):
     Name the estimated occupation based on activities in an RPG style.
     Examples:
     - AI Mage
     - Prompt Alchemist
     - Tech Sage
     - Digital Architect

STATUS PARAMETERS (Calculated based on Activity):
The following parameters should be quantified (1–99) based on actual activity status:

- LV (Level):
  Calculated from years of activity × posting frequency × consistency

- INT (Intelligence):
  Calculated from technical posts, frequency of specialized terminology use, and abstraction level of thought

- TEC (Technical Skill):
  Calculated from the volume of AI / development / research / implementation output

- CRE (Creativity):
  Calculated from the frequency of unique perspectives, prompt design, and idea posts

- COM (Communication Power):
  Calculated from follower count, engagement, and post diffusion power

- EXP (Experience Points):
  Comprehensive evaluation of past achievements, projects, and continuous activities

* Ensure each value maintains a relative balance typical of an RPG (not all stats should be uniform).

UI & TYPOGRAPHY:
- Font should be a dot font (8-bit RPG style).
- Text must be perfectly legible, without blurring or distortion.
- Primarily use Japanese UI, with some English status names concurrently used.

COLOR PALETTE:
- Use a color palette extracted from the attached image (Image 1).
- Composed using a limited color count reminiscent of 16-bit RPGs.
- Background should be dark-toned, with high contrast for text and gauges.

GRAPHIC ELEMENTS:
- Status bars displayed as pixel gauges.
- Icons (sword, book, gear, star, etc.) should also be unified in dot art.
- UI borders and dividers should be in the style of an RPG menu.

COMPOSITION:
- The overall composition should be a “screenshot of an in-game status screen.”
- While mindful of business card size ratio, maintain high information density.
- Lay a faint dot pattern or grid in the background.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Name, handle, and URL must be accurately and exactly displayed.
- Exclude meaningless strings or distorted characters.
- Strictly adhere to the pixel art style; photo-realism is prohibited.
- Maintain a consistent world view as an RPG UI.
- Dynamically reconstruct based on the input {argument name="argument" default="argument"} and Image 1.
```

## Victorian Gothic Royal Portrait Magazine Cover Prompt

> Sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Generate a 9:16 vertical, realistic photo of a "Victorian Gothic Royal" portrait: Use the uploaded FACE_REF as the character identity reference, 100% restoring the facial features and bone structure; the person is wearing heavy-duty court attire in the style of the "Victorian Era (19th Century) Court Gown" (corset, large crinoline, lace/velvet, royal jewelry), photographed next to the Tower Bridge or the Palace of Westminster (Big Ben) in London. The image should have photography, styling, and cover design quality comparable to 'Harper’s Bazaar', maintaining the magazine's consistent design style (Chinese and English design).

【INPUTS | Input】
FACE_REF: Uploaded facial feature reference image (identity lock)

【ABSOLUTE PRIORITY | Identity Lock (Highest Priority)】
100% restoration of FACE_REF's facial features and bone structure: Eye distance, nose bridge and wings, lip shape, jawline, and cheekbone structure must be completely consistent, with no deviation.
Realistic skin texture: Visible subtle textures and pores; avoid excessive smoothing or 'influencer' filters.
Adult female image (adult).

【SHOT TYPE | Two View Options (Model randomly selects one, must be realistic and consistent)】
A) Half-body cover: From the chest to above the waist, with jewelry, lace neckline, and eyes being the clearest (Primary recommendation)
B) Full-body cover: Complete presentation from head to toe, showing the full silhouette of the huge crinoline combined with the London landmark, strong perspective (Alternative)
(Regardless of A/B: Must maintain a "cover composition," with white space reserved for typography, but without torn paper/hand-drawn effects.)

【LOCATION | Scene (London, UK)】
London Landmark: By the River Thames, with the magnificent Gothic architecture of the Palace of Westminster (Big Ben) or the Tower of London Bridge in the background.
Weather option (random): 1) London Fog (classic foggy texture, soft and mysterious light) 2) Just after rain (wet ground reflection, lead-gray sky)
Clean background: No tourists, no modern signs, no mobile phone status bar UI, no watermarks or subtitles.

【LIGHTING & CAMERA | Photographic Quality (Magazine Grade)】
Lens feel: 85mm portrait quality (half-body) or 50mm/70mm (full-body), shallow depth of field, eyes are the sharpest.
Lighting: Overcast diffused light + dramatic fill light (emphasizing facial structure); diamonds/gems must have realistic fire; velvet texture must be deep.
Color: High-end cinematic feel, low-saturation blue-gray environment combined with the dark tones of the clothing (red/blue/black) to form a Gothic aesthetic; overall clean, transparent, and luxurious.

【WARDROBE | Victorian Court Gown (Heavy-duty, Sculpting, Intricate Details)】
Target aesthetic: "Wasp waist, large skirt, layered lace, heavy velvet, crown, queenly aura."

Silhouette and Layers (X-shaped tailoring)
Upper body: Highly structured corset, square or high neckline, puff sleeves or leg-of-mutton sleeves
Lower body: Huge crinoline or bustle, with noticeable stacking design at the back waist, skirt trailing on the ground
Decoration: Abundant ruffles, bows, dangling tassels
Fabric and Craftsmanship (Heavy craftsmanship must be "visible")
Main fabric: Heavy velvet, taffeta, Chantilly Lace
Main techniques: Beading, gold thread embroidery, ribbon weaving
Pattern themes: Damask, roses, thistle (symbol of Scotland), paisley pattern
Details: Lace gloves, neck ornament (Choker), medal/brooch
Headwear (Royal Jewelry Headpiece)
Core structure: Victorian small crown (Tiara) or feather headpiece
Decoration: Diamonds, sapphires, pearls
Earrings/Necklace: Extremely exaggerated diamond necklace, drop earrings

【COLOR MATRIX | Color Matrix Pairing (Must be executed, and must be "Royal Dark Colors")】
(Deep luxury of the late Victorian era)
Randomly select 1 set from the following three "Main Color Schemes" for the [Base Color/Large Area Main Fabric Color].

Scheme G (Royal Blue Base):
Base Color: Deep sapphire blue/midnight blue (velvet texture)
Embroidery/Decoration: Silver lace, diamonds, sapphires
Scheme A (Victorian Red Base):
Base Color: Wine red/Burgundy red (deep passion)
Embroidery/Decoration: Black lace, gold embroidery, rubies
Scheme R (Mourning Black Base):
Base Color: Ink black (Black Jet, extremely Gothic)
Embroidery/Decoration: Black beads, black lace, small amounts of gold or silver highlights

【Color Execution Rules】
Colors must be deep and rich, reflecting the majesty of the "Empire on which the sun never sets" and Gothic mystery.

【POSE | Cover Pose (Majestic, Stiff, Queenly Aura)】
Half-body: Chin slightly raised, cold gaze, one hand gently touching the necklace
Full-body: Standing tall (like a portrait painting), hands clasped on the crinoline, overwhelming presence
Expression: Serious, arrogant, imperious; absolutely no smiling (Victorian style).

【OUTPUT | Output】
1 vertical 9:16 realistic magazine cover photo
Random: Half-body or Full-body (A/B selection)
Random: Fog or Rain (Weather selection)
Random: Sapphire Blue / Wine Red / Ink Black (Color matrix selection)
Styling: Victorian court gown direction + Crown/Choker + Corset and large crinoline + Velvet and lace
```

[Global Instruction]: A 3x3 grid collage, all 9 panels must feature the exact same female character. Strictly maintain facial features consistent with the reference image. Do not change her facial features, only change her expression, makeup, and styling to match each movie theme.
Panel 1: '{argument name="movie 1" default="Love Actually"}' style - Airport farewell scene, wearing a Santa hat, holding a wrapped gift, warm embrace, background features a giant airport Christmas tree and string lights, soft lighting.
Panel 2: '{argument name="movie 2" default="Home Alone"}' style - Surprised, exaggerated expression covering her face, wearing a crooked Santa hat, background features a home Christmas tree, gift piles, and decorative lights, comedic effect.
Panel 3: '{argument name="movie 3" default="The Polar Express"}' style - By the train window, wearing a sleeping cap, holding hot cocoa with a candy cane, magical snowy scenery and Christmas village outside the window, dreamy blue and gold tones.
Panel 4: '{argument name="movie 4" default="How the Grinch Stole Christmas"}' style - Real person, green-toned clothing with a red Santa hat, funny expression, holding stolen Christmas decorations, background features Christmas village lights and gifts, creative styling.
Panel 5: '{argument name="movie 5" default="Miracle on 34th Street"}' style - Vintage department store scene, wearing a classic red Christmas dress, holding Christmas shopping bags, background features a giant Christmas tree and vintage decorations, classic Hollywood lighting (center).
Panel 6: '{argument name="movie 6" default="Elf"}' style - Full elf costume (green top, red striped socks, pointed hat with a bell), joyful jumping posture, holding a candy cane, background features a candy-colored Christmas workshop.
Panel 7: '{argument name="movie 7" default="Frozen"}' style - Ice-blue princess dress with a snowflake crown, holding a magic ice crystal, snowflakes flying, background features an ice crystal castle and a large Christmas tree, special festive candies, Disney magic feel.
Panel 8: '{argument name="movie 8" default="True Lies"}' style - Black dress with red Christmas accessories, holding champagne, background features a Christmas party scene with string lights and decorative balls, elegant and mysterious.
Panel 9: '{argument name="movie 9" default="Last Christmas"}' style - Stylish woman wearing a red vintage coat with a green scarf and gold earrings, holding a Christmas gift box, standing under the Christmas street lights in London, background features the Covent Garden giant Christmas tree and festive window displays, warm night lighting, hopeful expression, modern romantic film aesthetic.
Each panel mimics the corresponding movie's color palette, lighting, and atmosphere, with extremely obvious Christmas decorative elements, movie poster composition, added cinematic title text effect, professional movie still quality, 4K high definition.

## K-Pop Idol Stage Portrait

> Stüdyo ışıklı portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
In a vertical composition crafted with a 2:3 aspect ratio, a vision of youthful elegance commands the spotlight, bathed in the ethereal, cool azure glow of a stage performance. The subject is a young woman, seemingly in the fresh bloom of her early twenties, whose porcelain fairness and delicate features gracefully reflect East Asian lineage, her skin possessing a radiant, flawless luminosity that gleams under the studio lights. Her visage is a study in supermodel symmetry, defined by large, expressive almond-shaped eyes that hold a captivating depth, a refined and slender nose, and soft, inviting lips tinted in a gentle rose hue, all framed by long, lustrous dark brown hair that cascades over her shoulders and is cut with airy bangs resting upon her forehead. She possesses a slender, lithe build, capturing a sense of delicate athleticism and poise as she stands upright, clutching a black microphone in her right hand, raised intimately to her lips in the midst of song. Her attire accentuates her petite frame, featuring a pristine {argument name="top color" default="white"} strapless tube top that reveals a toned midriff and delicate collarbones, paired with a vibrant, high-waisted {argument name="skirt color" default="pink"} pleated skirt that flares playfully about her hips, adding a pop of youthful color to the scene. To her left, a lens flare from a stage light bursts like a star, casting a rim of light along her silhouette and hair, while the background dissolves into a soft, focused blur of deep blues and purples, ensuring her radiant figure remains the undisputed focal point of this harmonious and aesthetically sublime portrait.
```

## Hyperrealistic Miniature Planet Cityscape

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a hyperrealistic miniature planet showcasing {argument name="city" default="Shanghai"} with famous landmarks seamlessly curving around the spherical surface. Position bold 3D white text ”{argument name="city" default="Shanghai"}” naturally integrated across the lush green central parkland with realistic shadows and dimensional depth. Capture from a top-down orbiting angle that emphasizes the dramatic planet curvature. Use soft golden hour daylight filtering through partly cloudy skies, casting gentle shadows on emerald grass and surrounding trees. The background should blend into a swirling atmospheric sky. Apply vibrant greens, warm earth tones, and soft blues. Render in polished photorealistic style with fine architectural detail. 1080x1080 format.
```

## Pulp Comic Style Poster Design with Multiple Characters Prompt

> Logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

Include these three people: [Image 1] [Image 2] [Image 3]. Ensure that the touch and posing of all characters maintain complete consistency.

A depiction and layout where the characters boldly jump out. Breaking through the panels.
Poster design themed around "Punk," "Comic Style," and "Picaresque (Villainous).".

Bold color scheme: Red (#D91419), black, white, and accent yellow.

Geometric distortion: Not squares, but shapes that are skewed or jaggedly cut (Clip-path).

Dynamic movement: A sense of dynamism, as if constantly animated, not a still image.

Typography: Bold, impactful sans-serif fonts (such as Impact or Anton) placed with varying size and angle.
Japanese fonts must have the size and angle of each character boldly changed. Decorated Gothic font.

Output in 16:9 aspect ratio.
The theme is a {argument name="theme" default="promotional poster for a major original art exhibition"}. Include five catchy phrases that make people want to buy or visit, the {argument name="title" default="title of the major original art exhibition"}, and the {argument name="game software name" default="name and logo of the game software"} that inspired it.

Also include the date and location description.
```

It casts realistic deep shadows onto the aged paper below to show thickness.
The woman is tearing through the vintage newspaper background with her hands, stepping out of the page.

RIGHT SIDE (THE COLUMNS): Printed directly onto the aged paper background. The layout is strictly divided into TWO SEPARATE VERTICAL COLUMNS of black printed text.
COLUMN 1 (Inner Left): Simulated article analyzing the face. Format: BULLET POINTS list structure.
COLUMN 2 (Outer Right): Simulated article analyzing the outfit. Format: BULLET POINTS list structure.

## Red-Blue Double Exposure Effect

> Minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Apply an artistic red–blue double-exposure effect using two poses of the same woman. Keep the base layer as the original pose, and generate a second pose where her head angle, facial direction, or expression is slightly different, as if captured a moment earlier or later. Color the second pose in red, the base pose in cyan, and offset the layers to create a clean ghosting effect. Preserve skin texture, tattoos, and contrast while keeping the background minimal.
```

## Gothic Noir Vigilante Portrait (Batgirl Inspired)

```
{
  "prompt_title": "Gothic Noir Vigilante Portrait",
  "author": "Shreya Yadav",
  "positive_prompt": "A hyper-realistic, cinematic 35mm photograph of a young female vigilante inspired by Batgirl. The subject has an ideally built, athletic physique, pale skin with detailed pores, and sharp, piercing green eyes. She has short, reddish-black hair, partially tucked messily under a sculpted bat-shaped mask. She is in the middle of an action, adjusting her mask with both hands, pulling it tight against her face. She is wearing a dark, textured leather bodysuit with a worn, scratched yellow bat emblem on the chest. The suit features gothic details, metallic stitching, and subtle battle scars. She wears dark yellow, weathered tactical gloves. The lighting is somber and dramatic, characterized by deep, heavy shadows and soft, cool blue edge lighting that outlines the contours of her face and suit. The background is a subdued gothic setting, featuring dark, foggy atmosphere and hints of shadowy architecture. The image evokes an intense, grounded, gothic noir aesthetic with high-contrast realism and a shallow depth of field.",
  "negative_prompt": "bright colors, cheerful, cartoon, anime, sketch, 3d render, plastic skin, smooth skin, makeup, lipstick, long hair, blonde hair, deformed hands, missing fingers, extra fingers, bad anatomy, floating objects, watermark, text, signature, blurry, low resolution, overexposed, distorted face, changing facial features, neon lights.",
  "parameters": {
    "aspect_ratio": "2:3",
    "resolution": "8k",
    "lighting_style": "Chiaroscuro, Cool Edge Lighting",
    "camera_type": "35mm Lens",
    "texture_quality": "High-fidelity leather and skin texture",
    "mood": "Somber, Intense, Gothic"
  },
  "midjourney_format": "/imagine prompt: A hyper-realistic portrait of a young female vigilante, Batgirl inspired, adjusting bat-shaped mask with both hands, pale skin, sharp green eyes, short reddish-black hair, dark textured leather bodysuit, worn yellow bat emblem, dark yellow tactical gloves, gothic details, metallic stitching, battle scars --ar 2:3 --stylize 250 --v 6.1 --style raw",
  "stable_diffusion_tags": [
    "hyper-realistic",
    "photorealistic",
    "8k",
    "raw photo",
    "vigilante",
    "batgirl-inspired",
    "short reddish-black hair",
    "green eyes",
    "pale skin",
    "leather texture",
    "weathered armor",
    "tactical gloves",
    "gothic noir",
    "volumetric fog",
    "dramatic shadows",

}

## Realistic Axonometric Miniature Model with Narrative Abstract Background

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Work Information Retrieval]
- Target Work Name: “{argument name="Work Name" default="Name to be added"}”
- Task: Retrieve the plot, setting, and narrative structure of this film/TV series/novel.
- Goal: Automatically filter the most representative famous scenes or core locations, and extract their emotional core (e.g., sadness, hope, determination, reunion, destruction, tranquility).
[Main Scene Construction: 3D Axonometric Miniature Model]
- Present a realistic 3D miniature model in the center.
- Perspective: Axonometric / isometric view.
- Model Content:
- Architectural details realistically restored according to the original work
- Character actions and expressions conform to physics and narrative logic
- Environmental atmosphere realistically presented (storm, morning fog, sunset, firelight, etc.)
- Rendering Style: Physically-based rendering of realistic materials and light.
- Effect: The model is small but detailed and realistic, like a “miniature replica of a real scene, illuminated by exhibition-grade lighting.”
[Background Design: Narrative-Driven Realistic Abstract Space]
- Pure white or monochrome backgrounds are prohibited.
- The background is constructed as a realistic, abstract emotional space based on the “scene's emotional core,” including:
① Emotional Tones (e.g., sadness → cold blue-gray; hope → warm gold light; tension → dark contrast)
② Air Dynamics and Volumetric Light (light fog, dust particles, backlighting, etc., carrying narrative meaning)
③ Lightweight Symbolic Elements (petals, confetti, ashes, stardust, water ripples, etc., as emotional particles)
④ Spatial Depth (aerial perspective, soft focus, gradient light creating a deep void with “lingering flow”)
- Goal: The background uses abstract light and shadow to hint at the context before and after the story, forming an emotional resonance with the main scene.
[Bottom Layout: Work Name + Classic Quote]
- Centered layout at the bottom.
- Content:
① Chinese Work Name: “{argument name="Work Name" default="Name to be added"}” (Font matches the work's style)
② A sentence or line from the original work related to the scene (Elegant serif font)
- The overall layout references an art museum plaque, simple, balanced, and textured.
[Overall Visual Requirements]
- Miniature model is realistic, precise, and has authentic materials.
- Background enhances the narrative with emotional colors, volumetric light, and symbolic particles.
- The entire image presents the realism and poetic atmosphere of a high-end, exhibition-grade narrative artwork.
Direct output, size 16:9.
```

## 3D Pointillism Sculpture from Christmas Baubles

> 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Based on the reference image, render the scene as a 3D pointillism sculpture made entirely of countless tiny Christmas tree ornaments (baubles). The spheres should have varied finishes: some metallic, some matte, and some with glitter, catching and reflecting festive light realistically. They are compacted tightly to form the shapes and color gradients of the original subject. A detailed photographic style highlighting the physical depth and texture of individual ornaments.
```

## Universal JSON Template for Editorial Product Ads

> Minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "project": "Nano Banana - Universal Product Editorial Ad",
  "version": "1.1",

  "input_requirements": {
    "reference_product_image": {
      "upload_required": true,
      "usage": "extract visual characteristics, color palette, possible target user, and implied purpose"
    },
    "branding_elements": {
      "allowed": false,
      "description": "no visible logos, trademarks, or brand names in the generated result"
    }
  },

  "image_specification": {
    "meta": {
      "clean_generation": true,
      "distraction_free": true
    }
  },

  "creative_directive": {
    "freedom_level": "full_creative_imagination",
    "Nano_Banana_role": "interpret product essence to design the rest of the scene",
    "editorial_intent": "produce a visually compelling advertisement based solely on the product reference"
  },

  "scene_generation": {
    "environment": {
      "type": "indoor_or_outdoor",
      "chosen_by": "best_use_case_for_product",
      "lighting": {
        "selection_method": "depends_on_product_use_case",
        "goal": [
          "enhance product visibility",
          "create a clean editorial mood",
          "support emotional impact"
        ]
      }
    },

    "subject_character": {
      "identity": "derived_from_product_reference",
      "gender": "male_or_female",
      "attributes_selection": [
        "style_inspired_by_product_color_palette",
        "personality_inferred_from_product_category",
        "age_and_vibe_matching_intended_audience"
      ],
      "pose": "chosen_by_creative_engine",
      "interaction_with_product": "natural and meaningful"
    },

    "product_presentation": {
      "visibility": "clear",
      "placement": "hero_position",
      "integration": "harmonized with entire composition"
    }
  },

  "ad_copy": {
    "text": {
      "placement": "editorial_style_overlay",
      "tone": "clean, modern, descriptive",
      "content": "short metaphorical line describing the product’s essence and purpose without naming a brand"
    }
  },
  "render_style": {
    "aesthetic": "editorial_magazine_ad",
    "resolution": "high_detail",
    "color_grading": "optimized_based_on_product_visuals",
    "medium": "photorealistic"
  }
}
```

## Snow Globe Christmas Scene Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Hyper-realistic and fantastical Christmas scene, a giant transparent snow globe (1.5m tall) placed on fresh snow outdoors.
* Inside the dome:
    * Model: Use [reference image], maintaining face, expression, hairstyle, and clothing.
    * Clothing: Use from the reference image.
    * Pose: Sitting cross-legged, resting her chin on her right hand, gaze directed to the upper right (upper left of the image).
    * Surroundings: Small wrapped gifts in red, gold, and green scattered on a sparkling white pedestal.
    * Effects: Artificial snow gently dances around her, and soft, warm fairy lights woven into the pedestal shine through the snow.
    * Pedestal Text: In a stylish font, write "{argument name="pedestal text" default="Merry Christmas"}".
* Outside the dome (Background):
    * Scenery: Snow-covered Christmas trees, decorated with ornaments matching the atmosphere of the generated image.
    * Atmosphere: Cold blue twilight sky, subtle bokeh effect. Background elements matching the atmosphere of the generated image.
    * Dome Surface: Light frost and snow accumulated on the top. Subtle reflections of light.
* Image Quality/Atmosphere:
    * Mood: High-key and magical atmosphere, mixing soft natural light and warm fairy light.
    * Details: Hyper-realistic 8K resolution, knit texture, individual snow crystals, glass refraction, skin glow.
    * Composition: Straight on, slight low angle, Shallow Depth of Field (Shallow DOF), cinematic winter mood.
```

## 3D Diorama Poster for 'Lord of the Mysteries'

> Minimal, lüks afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Please design a high-quality 3D poster for the film/novel "{argument name="name" default="Name to be added"}", requiring prior retrieval of information about the film/novel and famous scene clips. First, use your knowledge base to retrieve the content of this film/novel and find the most representative famous scene or core location. In the center of the image, construct this scene as an exquisite isometric 3D miniature model.

The style should adopt the delicate, soft rendering style of Dreamworks animation. You need to restore the architectural details, character dynamics, and environmental atmosphere of the time; whether it's a storm or a tranquil afternoon, it should naturally blend into the model's lighting and shadows. Regarding the background, do not use a simple pure white base. Please create a void environment around the model with faint ink wash blending and flowing light mist, with elegant color tones, making the image appear breathable and deep, highlighting the preciousness of the central model.

Finally, for the layout at the bottom, generate Chinese text. Center the name of the novel, with a font design that matches the style of the original work. Below the title, automatically retrieve and lay out a classic description or line from the original work related to that scene, using an elegant serif font. The overall layout should be exquisitely balanced, like a high-end museum collection plaque.
```

## Time-Warp Cinematic Split Scene Prompt

> Sinematik, retro mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A horizontally split cinematic shot showing the seamless fusion of the 【{argument name="Scene" default="Fudan University Campus"}】 in two distinct eras: the left side of the image is 【{argument name="Era A" default="100 years ago"}】, and the right side is 【{argument name="Era B" default="Present"}】 (default contrast is approximately 100 years ago vs. contemporary). 

On the left side of the image (【Era A】):
Present architecture, interior or environmental design, materials, transportation, and props consistent with that historical period, with all details clearly belonging to that specific time. Figures wear attire completely consistent with 【Era A】, including hairstyles, accessories, and typical handheld items (such as books, umbrellas, musical instruments, letters, newspapers, etc.). The overall atmosphere is full of nostalgia, with authentic and delicate historical realism.

On the right side of the image (【Era B】):
Show the modern version of the same 【Scene】. Architecture is updated or renovated, using modern materials (glass, steel, LED screens, modern furniture), equipped with modern vehicles or equipment, and current technology products (smartphones, laptops, cameras, etc.). The figures' dressing style is completely consistent with current trends.

In the central area of the image:
The elements of the two eras naturally converge and merge, without a clear, hard dividing line. Figures from 【Era A】 and 【Era B】 visually interact: people from different eras look at each other, traverse each other's space, or show expressions of surprise at the technology and objects of the other era. Architecture and environment achieve smooth transformation, for example: an ancient stone gate gradually evolves into a modern campus entrance, classical concert hall decorations slowly transform into a futuristic stage, and old street shops gradually become neon-lit modern stores.

Ensure that the scene is not just a simple left-right comparison, but a dynamic cross-era interactive experience, where the architecture, clothing, props, and figures' actions clearly reflect the contrast and fusion of the two eras. The image style is photorealistic, 8K ultra-high resolution, cinematic lighting effects, wide-angle view, rich material details, possessing a strong sense of time-travel narrative.
```

## Cyber-Grunge Surveillance Fashion Editorial Prompt (Japanese Woman)

> Sinematik, minimal arayüz tasarımı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cyber-grunge surveillance-style fashion editorial featuring a young, beautiful, cute Japanese woman in her early 20s, with large expressive eyes and K-pop idol–inspired makeup. Her makeup highlights flawless radiant skin, soft shimmer around the eyelids, sharp eyeliner, and glossy lips, giving a refined feminine glow.

She has long, sleek black hair with perfectly trimmed bangs, smooth texture, and soft highlights, flowing naturally behind her as she walks.

Her outfit consists of a fitted rib-texture turtle-neck knit top and denim short pants with visible stitching details, slightly low-rise styling, giving a modern Japanese Y2K street flavor. She wears long sleek boots—clean, structured, and stylish—adding elegance and contrast to her exposed legs. Accessorized with a small gold pendant necklace. She holds an iced chocolate/coffee cup with a straw in her left hand and a half-eaten pastry in her right hand.

Her body posture is mid-stride, full-body captured, facing slightly off-center, head angled down slightly with a cool, indifferent expression—visually as if caught candidly by a high-end surveillance system monitoring an urban location.

Scene environment:
A bright late-afternoon plaza with paved concrete tiles. Highly directional sunlight creates long diagonal shadows, deep blacks under the boots, and warm atmospheric highlights. The tone feels cinematic but raw, like real street documentation.

Technical image style:
Hyper-real 8K Ultra-HD clarity, deep focus across full frame, dramatic daylight contrast, color palette composed of urban greys, pure whites, deep blacks, and striking crimson UI overlay accents. 1440 × 1920 resolution, 4:5 editorial framing.

Layout structure:
Editorial collage combining：
● one large main full-body walking shot
＋
● three cropped secondary frames:
— close-up of face + makeup styling + drink hand
— torso crop featuring turtle-neck knit texture
— boot-and-legs detail with pavement shadows

These cropped windows connect visually via red surveillance-style vector lines.

UI Overlay Components:
· thin red bounding rectangles
· telemetry-like ID codes
· crosshair alignment marks
· vector targeting lines
· timecode print
· numeric encryption motifs

Example printed graphics:
“TR521”
“CCWW”
“18/02”
“#25747//”
“#83575//”
“19 5 3 21 18 9 20 25”

Atmospheric concept:
Modern cyber-grunge × candid street-watching × dystopian fashion documentation.
Feels like a digital observation archive rather than posed photography.
A stylish mystery aura, privacy-invasion chic, cinematic realism.

Camera Specifications:
Shot from a slightly high-angle perspective, around 35–50mm focal length, long daylight-cast shadows visible, crisp texture on concrete paving, deep-focus realism.
```

## Makoto Shinkai-style cinematic sky background illustration

> Sinematik illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Generate a beautiful background illustration in the style of Makoto Shinkai’s animated films. The core scene is: "{argument name="scene" default="a railway crossing in full bloom with cherry blossoms"}", but the focus is not on characters; it is on the environment and the atmosphere of the sky.

Use a wide 16:9 composition, as if it were a single frame taken directly from a Shinkai movie. The sky must be the absolute protagonist, occupying most of the frame. Paint an extremely spectacular, detail-rich sky: for example, a towering cumulonimbus cloud stretching to the heavens, or gorgeous evening clouds where orange, purple, and deep blue gradients interweave, or a huge rainbow arching over the city after the rain. The lighting should be at the most magical time of day, either Golden Hour or Blue Hour. Include strong yet soft lens flares in the sky, with sunlight streaming through gaps in the clouds to form clear volumetric rays, and tiny glowing dust motes and light particles drifting in the air.

For the ground and environment, render the {scene} in near photo-realistic detail: carefully depict utility poles, complex webs of power lines, road signs, traffic lights, vending machines, distant skyscraper silhouettes, and the small traces of everyday urban life. If the scene includes rain or puddles, the ground should look wet, reflecting the colors of the sky and nearby lights like a mirror, filling the image with a transparent, cool atmosphere and a sense of slight humidity.

In terms of composition, use a wide-angle, large-scale view to emphasize the grandeur of the environment and a strong sense of depth, making the viewer feel like they are looking up and being swallowed by the scenery. If there are characters in the image, they should be tiny silhouettes or back views, standing at a street corner, on a rooftop, or by the railway, gazing at the sky or the distance. Their small figures should enhance the sense of story, loneliness, and imagined future, without stealing the spotlight from the environment.

The overall color should be clear and saturated but not harsh. The contrast between light and shadow should be strong yet still soft, with enough detail that the image can be used directly as a desktop wallpaper. The style must clearly carry Shinkai’s signature sense of romance and transparency, rather than a heavy oil painting or pure photographic realism. High-resolution illustration, 16:9 widescreen ratio.
```

## Luxury minimalist product photography based on a reference image

> Minimal, lüks ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Using my uploaded reference image as the base, generate a minimalist, high-quality commercial still life photograph with the aesthetic of a luxury brand advertisement.

Preserve the general shape, proportions, and main color scheme of the core product in the reference so that it is instantly recognizable as the same product, but upgrade the overall texture and atmosphere of the image, making it look like an ad for a top-tier international fragrance, skincare item, or high-end electronic product.

Use a vertical 4:5 composition. The background should be a large area of clean, neutral negative space, such as soft off-white, cool gray, or light beige, creating a quiet, restrained, and expensive studio environment. Apart from the core product and a minimal number of necessary supports, do not add any extra decorations. No clutter, no elaborate scene setup—only keep very simple geometric supports (such as a plain white cube, a cylinder, or a thin transparent panel). The supports must be simple and low-profile, existing only to hold and accentuate the product.

Strongly emphasize the material details of the product, and let the lighting fully serve those materials:
Show frosted glass with a fine, soft matte texture, ceramics with a matte or slightly glazed feel, and polished metal with clean, sharp specular highlights. Liquids should have clearly visible viscosity and volume. Avoid any cheap plastic feel, complex patterns, or gaudy stickers; the overall material quality must feel cool, restrained, and luxurious.

Use professional studio lighting: mainly soft, diffused light from softboxes, plus precise rim light outlining the product’s shape. The contours should be crisp and layered, and the shadows should be soft and clean, with no noise or dirty shadow patches. You may use a near-macro viewpoint, moving in closer to the product to highlight materials and details, while preserving ample negative space around the frame so the product can "speak for itself" in the emptiness.

Keep the overall color scheme neutral and calm. The product’s own color can be slightly more saturated, making it the single visual focal point in the scene. Do not use exaggerated gradient backgrounds, flashy colored lighting, or e-commerce promo aesthetics. No large-price text, labels, or cartoon graphics.

The final result must be a high-resolution commercial key visual that looks ready for a magazine back cover or a high-end brand announcement poster: minimalist, quiet, high-end, with the core product confidently centered, surrounded by clean negative space and precisely controlled lighting.
```

## Nano Banana Pro torn paper style-switching edit prompt

> Minimal, elle çizilmiş mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
task: "edit-image: add widened torn-paper layered effect"

base_image:
  use_reference_image: true
  preserve_everything:
    - character identity
    - facial features and expression
    - hairstyle and anatomy
    - outfit design and colors
    - background, lighting, composition
    - overall art style

rules:
  - Only modify the torn-paper interior areas.
  - Do not change pose, anatomy, proportions, clothing details, shading, or scene elements.

effects:
  - effect: "torn-paper-reveal"
    placement: "across chest height"
    description:
      - Add a wide, natural horizontal tear across the chest area.
      - The torn interior uses the style defined in `interior_style`.

  - effect: "torn-paper-reveal"
    placement: "lower abdomen height"
    description:
      - Add a wide horizontal tear across the lower abdomen.
      - The torn interior uses the style defined in `interior_style`.

interior_style:
  mode: {argument name="style mode" default="line-art"}

  style_settings:
    line-art:
      palette: "monochrome"
      line_quality: "clean, crisp"
      paper: "notebook paper with subtle ruled lines"

    sumi-e:
      palette: "black ink tones"
      brush_texture: "soft bleeding edges"
      paper: "plain textured paper"

    figure-render:
      material: "PVC-like"
      shading: "semi-realistic highlights"
      paper: "plain smooth surface"

    colored-pencil:
      stroke_texture: "visible pencil grain"
      palette: "soft layered hues"
      paper: "rough sketchbook paper"

    watercolor:
      palette: "soft transparent pigments"
      blending: "smooth bleeding"
      edges: "soft contours"
      paper: "watercolor paper texture"

    pencil-drawing:
      graphite_texture: "visible pencil grain"
      shading: "smooth gradients"
      line_quality: "mixed sharp and soft"
      tone: "gray-scale"
      paper: "notebook paper with faint ruled lines"
```

```
A {argument name="subject" default="young adult male"} leans casually against a weathered stone balustrade, his relaxed shoulders and shifted weight conveying candid ease. He is dressed in a {argument name="suit color" default="light grey"} tailored suit paired with a deep navy, unbuttoned open-collared shirt. His hair is cleanly slicked back with product, catching a gentle sheen. Behind aviator sunglasses, his gaze is focused sharply off-camera to the left, accompanied by a serious expression with his mouth slightly ajar as if caught mid-breath. His hands are loosely clasped at his waist; the left hand rests with fingers wrapped lightly over the right, its thumb extended forward, displaying a rectangular silver dress watch with a light blue-grey leather strap, while only the thumb and index knuckle of the right hand remain visible beneath. He is positioned in a deeply layered {argument name="location" default="outdoor lakeside terrace setting"}. In the bottom left foreground, vibrant crimson red geraniums and dark foliage burst from a worn black metal planter box on a railing. To his lower right in the midground, the weathered warm off-white plastered stone pillar, detailed with subtle scuffs and matte grey undertones, supports a classic stone urn planter filled with green shrubbery and a solitary white flower. Stretching out behind him, pristine slate blue lake water ripples with choppy surface texture, revealing a small white boat in the far-left distance. Rising steeply in the deep background are mountains densely carpeted in thriving forest green trees, heavily interspersed with small white houses and villas clinging to the hillside. The scene exudes a sophisticated, quiet luxury, bathed in cool, moody natural light from an overcast sky acting as a giant softbox. Diffused lighting pours gently from above, creating a low-contrast illumination that casts faint, soft-edged shadows beneath his suit lapels, under his hands, and provides subtle modeling on the right side of his face. Crisp specular highlights form soft reflections on the aviator lenses and cast a gentle sheen on the watch face. The color palette relies on desaturated cool greys, slate blues, and forest greens, sharply punctuated by the vivid crimson accents. Captured as tack-sharp, grain-free realistic digital editorial photography from a straight-on perspective, utilizing a 50mm to 85mm lens at an aperture between f/4 and f/5.6, creating a medium depth of field that keeps the subject crisply in focus while atmospheric haze slightly softens the distant mountains. Cinematic post-processing applies cool color grading, desaturates the greens and blues, lifts the black point for a matte finish, and selectively enhances the red floral tones, all beautifully framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

## Building Projection Night Portrait

> Afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the attached photo as an identity reference only. Keep the person's recognizable facial structure, eye shape, nose, lips, jawline, skin tone, hairline and hair characteristics, and their apparent adult age. Do not copy the clothing, background, pose or lighting from the reference. A night photograph of a {argument name="setting" default="city square"}. The flat side wall of a {argument name="building type" default="six storey brick building"} fills most of the frame. A projector on a rooftop opposite throws an enormous portrait of the adult woman onto that wall: head and shoulders, roughly four storeys tall, three quarter view, calm expression, eyes looking slightly down and to the left as if she is watching the square below. The projected portrait behaves like light on brick, not like a printed poster. It dims and warps where it crosses window recesses, a drainpipe and a fire escape. It breaks and skips across the mortar lines. Its edges fall off softly at the corners of the wall. There is a slight keystone distortion because the projector is not level with the wall. Down on the pavement at the bottom of the frame, small, the same adult woman stands with her back three quarters to the camera, hands in the pockets of a {argument name="outfit" default="long dark coat"}, head tilted up, looking at her own projected face. She is about one fifteenth the height of the projected head. Environment: wet paving stones, a bicycle chained to a post, a closed kiosk, one other pedestrian walking out of frame, a bus stop sign, the glow of a street lamp. Camera: standing on the pavement about twenty five metres back, phone at chest height tilted slightly up, vertical 9:16 frame, the building filling the top three quarters and the small figure placed low and left of centre. Light: the projector is the brightest thing in the scene. Cold street lamps and a red traffic signal reflecting in the wet stone. Her small figure is lit only by projector spill and street lamp, so her face is barely readable and that is correct. Realism: dust and haze visible in the projector beam high above, brick texture showing through the projected image, slight flicker banding, a soft blue night sky with no stars, grain in the shadows. The projection must read as light thrown onto a rough wall, not as a poster stuck to it. The large projected face must clearly be the same person as the small figure below. Do not add a second projected image. Do not clean the wall.
```

He bears a serious, candidly authentic expression with relaxed eyebrows, his eyes looking away to the right of the frame. He leans heavily backward and to the right against a waist-high structure, his body turned forty-five degrees relative to the support, with legs casually crossed and shoulders subtly tilted downward on the leaning side. His right hand rests naturally atop the rounded edge of the structure, fingers draped loosely over the dark metal. He is positioned on a grey and brown loose gravel pathway bounded by a flat grey stone border in the foreground. He leans against an ornate, weathered dark brown and black cast-iron railing featuring vertical bars and decorative central medallions, accented by bright green climbing ivy intertwining near its base. Beyond the railing unfolds a {argument name="location" default="deep, scenic European lake vista"}, featuring calm, pristine, dark greenish-blue waters in the lower right. The expansive background reveals towering dark green Mediterranean cypress columns, lush forested hills, and classic vintage stucco villas painted in warm pale yellow and peach, topped with terracotta orange tiled roofs and shuttered windows, including a boathouse nestled directly on the shoreline. The mood is serene, calm, and sophisticated, bathed in soft, diffused natural daylight filtering from an overcast sky above. This low-contrast, moderate-intensity light casts short, gradual grey shadows beneath his shoes and subtle shading under the railing, while gently pulling a soft sheen from his hair and natural highlights from his skin. The split-complementary color palette elegantly balances the navy and khaki wardrobe against the deep forest greens and terracotta architectural accents. Captured with an 85mm lens at an aperture between f/4.0 and f/5.6 at ISO 100, the straight-on shot features a tack-sharp subject isolated against a slightly soft yet fully recognizable background, finished with a subtle color grade leaning toward classic film tones that slightly desaturates bright colors for an organic, clean aesthetic, all framed in a 3:4 aspect ratio.

## Mediterranean Yacht Club Editorial

> Retro, lüks görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A young adult male with dark curly hair sits casually at a 45-degree angle towards the right, gazing downward through geometric sunglasses with a serious, focused, and candid expression. He is dressed in a fitted warm gray long-sleeve crewneck shirt, maintaining relaxed, perfectly level shoulders. His right hand holds the top right edge of a dark open menu, his thumb resting naturally on the front cover pointing slightly upward while his fingers remain organically tucked behind. The scene unfolds on a waterfront restaurant patio, where he sits straight on at eye level behind a wooden table in the immediate foreground. This table arrangement features an empty pristine clear wine glass catching bright light reflections, a small worn light-wood traditional pepper grinder, a new clear glass olive oil bottle containing dark golden oil, a white plate with a cloth napkin, a vintage woven wicker chair back exhibiting a light tan and pale blue-green pattern, and a smooth dark olive-green pot blooming with vibrant white Kalanchoe flower clusters. Behind him, a worn silver metallic patio umbrella pole extends vertically out of frame, giving way to a deep, gently soft background revealing a Mediterranean seaside environment with distant landmasses under a cloudy blue summer sky, alongside massive multi-deck white luxury motor yachts featuring dark tinted windows and radar domes resting on deep ocean blue waters. The atmosphere is illuminated by bright, highly directional hard natural sunlight striking from the front and top right at a 45-degree angle, bathing the scene in a warm golden light with absolutely no ambient fill. This intense single light source creates an energetic, highly saturated, and high-contrast environment, casting short, deep black, harsh-edged shadows under his chin, onto his cheeks from his sunglasses, and beneath the foreground objects, while preserving crisp specular highlights on his textured hair, shoulders, the rims of the glassware, and the brilliant white yachts. The luxurious, breezy seaside dining aesthetic is captured as a medium-shot editorial digital photograph with a clean, structured visual style and a complementary palette emphasizing deep ocean blue, warm gray, crisp white, and warm wood tones. Shot with a 35mm lens at f/4 and a fast 1/1000s shutter speed at ISO 100 to ensure tack-sharp focus on the subject while retaining context in the distinct but naturally blurred background, the image utilizes post-processing that enhances blue and white saturation, slightly lifts shadows to maintain the dark shirt's detail, and warms the overall white balance to emphasize the sunny Mediterranean atmosphere, all perfectly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

A young adult male stands confidently in a candid, relaxed street style pose, turned slightly at a 45-degree angle with his weight centered and shoulders level. He has thick, messy wavy dark hair and an authentic expression featuring a neutral mouth and calm eyes gazing naturally off-camera. He is dressed elegantly in a tailored {argument name="suit color" default="charcoal grey"} suit paired with a crisp white dress shirt, a black textured knit tie, and a crisp white pocket square. His left arm is bent at the elbow, with his hand resting gently at waist level to lightly hold the edge of his suit jacket near the button, naturally revealing a {argument name="watch style" default="square watch with a leather strap"}. His right hand rests comfortably at his lower waist, casually holding black sunglasses by the temples pointed downward. Above him, a thriving canopy of {argument name="flower type" default="bright pink bougainvillea"} branches and green leaves hangs down like a natural frame in the foreground. He stands on a light gray concrete walkway next to a strip of green grass. To his left in the midground rises a weathered historic stone building facade constructed of cut stone blocks, featuring an arched doorway, a multi-paned wooden window frame, and a vintage black metal glass-paned wall lantern. The building's wall surface is a rough, matte plaster displaying patchy warm beige and sand colors with dark gray undertones and weathered cracks. To his right, a worn black wrought iron balcony railing projects outward. The background gently blurs into medium spatial depth, revealing a distant catering table draped in a new white tablecloth topped with dark bottles. The scene is bathed in bright, soft diffused natural daylight from above with an ambient bounce, casting a sophisticated warm golden glow over the European summer afternoon. Shadows feature soft, gradual gray edges, deliberately placed under his chin, beneath the jacket lapels, and cast softly onto the stone wall behind him, while pristine specular highlights catch his forehead, cheekbones, the bridge of his nose, and the glass face of his watch. The overall color palette is a complementary, highly contrasting mix of dominant charcoal grey and warm sand, punctuated by pure white and bougainvillea pink accents. Captured digitally straight-on using an 85mm lens at f/2.8, 1/500s, and ISO 100, the photorealistic image employs a medium depth of field to keep the subject tack sharp against the blurred background with absolutely no grain. The final aesthetic features subtle warm color grading to emphasize the stone and floral elements, balanced shadow recovery to preserve intricate details within the dark suit, and crisp sharpening on the subject's textural features, seamlessly framed in a 9:16 aspect ratio.
```

```
A young adult male exudes confident masculinity, positioned centrally and leaning slightly to his right at a 45-degree angle against a vintage {argument name="moped color" default="navy blue"} moped. He features dark, slicked-back hair catching subtle specular highlights from styling product, and his expression is fixed in a calm, closed-mouth neutral state, his gaze directed off-camera to the left from behind dark rectangular sunglasses. He is dressed in a {argument name="outfit top" default="fitted light grey short-sleeve t-shirt"} and dark brown high-waisted pleated trousers. His left hand is casually tucked into his trouser pocket, exposing only his wrist and forearm resting against his hip, while his right hand rests lightly on the moped left handlebar, his relaxed fingers draped gently over the grip to reveal silver rings. In the immediate foreground, the navy blue Piaggio moped features a chrome headlight casing. Behind him unfolds a medium-depth vintage European cobblestone street, framed asymmetrically to balance his visual weight against the left side of the scene. To the left in the midground, there is a dominant vintage dark carved wood window frame, while two worn dark wood chairs with woven rush seats sit stacked beneath it. To the right, a worn dark brown paneled wooden door features a circular brass knocker and silver handle, adjacent to a weathered off-white matte plaster wall with patchy warm grey undertones, faded peach borders, and minor dirt accumulation, bearing an aged black cast iron wall-mounted post box with a crest emblem, all grounded by a dark charcoal grey concrete and cobblestone floor. The atmosphere is quiet, moody, and sophisticated, bathed in soft, diffused natural lighting spilling from a single large overcast sky source above. A confident young male model sits facing the camera on the left side of the frame, leaning slightly to his left with relaxed shoulders in an organic, composed posture. His medium-length, wavy hair, measuring roughly three to four inches on top, is swept back and styled with a matte product, featuring distinct natural flyaways beautifully illuminated by the sun. He holds a neutral expression with a closed mouth and a direct gaze through {argument name="sunglasses color" default="vibrant amber yellow"} sunglasses. He is dressed in a {argument name="shirt color" default="crisp, bright white"} shirt that intensely catches the direct sunlight, accessorized with a subtle earbud and a visible belt. His relaxed right hand rests naturally on his leg, revealing a sleek wristwatch. He is positioned against a {argument name="wall texture" default="weathered, warm gray plaster"} wall detailed with a rough, patchy matte finish and visible scuffs, contrasting with the dark gray stone cobblestones beneath him. The scene recedes with deep spatial depth into a street setting on the right, where a layered visual hierarchy draws the eye from the subject to the background. In the right midground, slightly out-of-focus worn cafe tables and black metal chairs sit beneath a weathered, striped fabric awning in the top right, while small, blurred figures of people dining populate the far background. The atmosphere captures a warm European summer afternoon, sculpted by a single, highly directional natural light source striking from a 45-degree side angle. This warm, golden hard light creates a dramatic, high-contrast chiaroscuro effect, producing blown-out highlights on his face and shirt while casting long, harsh-edged, deep black shadows across the textured wall behind him. The complementary color palette heavily balances the dominant whites and deep shadow browns with warm golden tans and the amber accent of his sunglasses, entirely devoid of ambient fill light. Captured straight-on in a tack-sharp, editorial lifestyle street photography style, the pristine, grain-free digital image utilizes a medium depth of field to isolate the subject. Enhanced with warm color grading and increased contrast at settings equivalent to f/2.8, 1/500s, and ISO 100, the composition perfectly applies the rule of thirds for an asymmetric, left-heavy balance, beautifully framed in a 3:4 aspect ratio.

The overall atmosphere should feel warm, peaceful, artistic, and upscale. ## Sophisticated Mediterranean Night Portrait

```
{argument name="subject" default="An adult male"} stands confidently with a 45-degree turn to the right, his weight centered and shoulders level, dressed in {argument name="clothing" default="a sleek, all-black button-down shirt and trousers"}. His deliberately posed expression exudes a calm sophistication, featuring relaxed eyebrows, a neutral, unsmiling mouth, and a gaze directed away from the camera. His relaxed left hand is tucked casually into his trouser pocket with the thumb resting outside, displaying a silver watch with a crisp white dial on his wrist, while his right hand holds a partially obscured black leather clutch at hip level. He is situated in a {argument name="setting" default="deep, layered Mediterranean-style courtyard at night"}, standing on a solid dark grey concrete floor. In the immediate left foreground, a thriving, dominant olive tree with a knotted, thick trunk and dense foliage anchors the asymmetric composition, while a pristine midground reflecting pool with dark water perfectly mirrors the environment below. In the background to the right, a pristine building facade rises, constructed of perfectly smooth, matte warm-white plaster that transitions in a subtle gradient, detailed with clean white columns, geometric cutouts, and recessed windows fitted with wooden lattice screens. An upscale evening ambiance permeates the space, defined by an analogous color palette of deep blacks, warm whites, and rich olive greens. Multiple artificial light sources bathe the scene in a warm, golden glow, combining uplighting at the base of the olive tree, moderately directional, diffused frontal hard light from above, and glowing interior architectural lights. This moody, high-contrast setup casts short, dramatic, deep black shadows under the man's jaw, behind the tree, and within the recessed architectural cutouts, while careful highlights are preserved on the subject's face, the metallic watch face, the textured tree trunk, and the smooth white columns. Captured straight on as realistic digital photography with a contemporary luxury editorial influence, the image utilizes a medium depth of field to render the background architecture slightly soft, finished with warm color grading, lifted shadows, and a grainy texture exhibiting moderate digital noise appropriate for low-light conditions, all seamlessly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

Capture a calm, thoughtful young woman in {argument name="age group" default="her early 20s"} with a slim build, fair skin with neutral undertones, an oval face, softly defined jawline, subtle cheekbones, a straight nose, natural lips with a muted coral tint, and straight dark eyebrows. The entire frame is filled with vibrant green grass, creating a clean and minimal outdoor setting. Shoot during daytime using soft natural sunlight from overhead, producing gentle directional shadows and a warm nostalgic atmosphere. Apply cinematic analog-inspired color grading with realistic film grain, rich olive green, black, white, and dark brown tones accented by warm amber highlights, silver metallic details, and natural skin tones. The final image should resemble a {argument name="overall aesthetic" default="premium retro fashion editorial"}, blending timeless film photography, quiet intellectual style, outdoor reading, minimal fashion, and nostalgic analog aesthetics in a refined, artistic composition.
```
